    @Override

        audioStateLabel.setText("");
        try {
            PreserveSystemAudioObservationFeature.StopOutcome result = get();

                throw new IOException("Audio session target changed unexpectedly.");
            }
            if (result.finalized()) {
                if (Objects.equals(result.folder(), currentFolder) && Objects.equals(result.note(), currentNote)) {
                    loadCurrentNote();
                    selectElementById(result.elementId());

                showStatus("РђСѓРґРёРѕ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅРѕ в†’ " + result.folder() + " / " + result.note());

            }
            refreshTrackedAudioIfVisible();
            String path = result.retainedWav() == null ? "вЂ”" : result.retainedWav().toAbsolutePath().normalize().toString();
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(MainHelperWindow.this, result.warning() + "\n\nР¤Р°Р№Р» СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ:\n" + path + "\n\nRecording AudioElement РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»РµРЅ РЅРµР·Р°РІРµСЂС€С‘РЅРЅС‹Рј.", "gd-cap вЂ” WAV СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
